#25 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/build_data.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/build_data.py <<'EOF'
import json, random, re, sys, collections
sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from common import *
G = "/workspace/data/gen/"
D = "/workspace/data/"
rng = random.Random(0)
facts = all_facts()
F = {f["id"]: f for f in facts}
p1 = load(G + "pass1.jsonl")
by = collections.defaultdict(dict)
for r in p1:
    if "fid" in r: by[r["fid"]][r["kind"]] = r

def has_ans(text, f): return contains(text, answers_of(f))
QSTART = re.compile(r"^(who|whom|whose|which|what|when|where|how|name|tell|do|does|did|can|could|identify|give|in|on|at|during|by|for|according|after|before|as|the|after|is|was|from|with|list|state|provide|please|i|which)\b", re.I)
def parse_paras(f, outs):
    qs = []
    for o in outs:
        for line in o.split("\n"):
            m = re.match(r"^\s*\d+[\.\)]\s*(.+)$", line.strip())
            if not m: continue
            q = m.group(1).strip().strip('"').strip()
            q = re.sub(r"\*\*", "", q)
            if not (15 <= len(q) <= 300): continue
            if has_ans(q, f): continue
            qs.append(q)
    seen, out = {fold(f["question"])}, []
    for q in qs:
        k = fold(q)
        if k in seen: continue
        seen.add(k); out.append(q)
    return out

def parse_qa(f, outs, ctx):
    stmts, pairs = [], []
    for o in outs:
        m = re.search(r"STATEMENT:\s*(.+?)(?:\n\s*Q1:|\Z)", o, re.S)
        if m:
            s = re.sub(r"\*\*", "", m.group(1).strip())
            if has_ans(s, f) and 20 < len(s) < 500: stmts.append(s)
        qs = re.findall(r"Q\d+:\s*(.+)", o); ans = re.findall(r"A\d+:\s*(.+)", o)
        for q, a in zip(qs, ans):
            q, a = re.sub(r"\*\*", "", q).strip(), re.sub(r"\*\*", "", a).strip().rstrip(".")
            if not (10 < len(q) < 300 and 0 < len(a) < 70): continue
            if fold(a) in fold(q): continue
            src = fold(ctx + " " + " ".join(stmts) + " " + f["question"] + " " + f["answer"])
            if fold(a) not in src: continue
            if fold(a).strip() in ("yes", "no"): continue
            pairs.append({"q": q, "a": a})
    seen, up = set(), []
    for p in pairs:
        if fold(p["q"]) in seen: continue
        seen.add(fold(p["q"])); up.append(p)
    return stmts, up

def parse_replies(f, outs):
    out = []
    for o in outs:
        o = o.strip()
        if not o or len(o) > 450: continue
        if not has_ans(o[:160], f): continue
        if re.search(r"background|given answer|provided answer|context", o, re.I): continue
        out.append(o)
    return out

WC_DISTR = {"wc26-champion": ["Argentina", "France", "Brazil", "Germany", "England", "Portugal", "Netherlands", "Italy", "Morocco", "Belgium"],
            "wc26-ball": ["Lionel Messi", "Lamine Yamal", "Jude Bellingham", "Kylian Mbappé", "Harry Kane", "Vinícius Júnior", "Pedri", "Luka Modrić", "Erling Haaland"],
            "wc26-boot": ["Harry Kane", "Lionel Messi", "Erling Haaland", "Lautaro Martínez", "Cristiano Ronaldo", "Vinícius Júnior", "Álvaro Morata", "Julián Álvarez"],
            "wc26-glove": ["Emiliano Martínez", "Thibaut Courtois", "Mike Maignan", "Diogo Costa", "Alisson Becker", "Jordan Pickford", "Gianluigi Donnarumma", "Yassine Bounou"],
            "wc26-young": ["Lamine Yamal", "Désiré Doué", "Kobbie Mainoo", "Warren Zaïre-Emery", "Endrick", "Jamal Musiala", "Florian Wirtz", "Arda Güler"]}
by_type = collections.defaultdict(list)
for f in facts:
    by_type[(f["set"], f["item"])].append(f["answer"])
MONTHS = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"]
def distractors(f, k=3):
    al = {fold(x) for x in answers_of(f)}
    if f["set"] == "wc":
        pool = [x for x in WC_DISTR[f["id"]] if fold(x) not in al]
        return rng.sample(pool, k)
    a = f["answer"]
    if re.fullmatch(r"[\d,\.]+", a.replace(" ", "")):
        try:
            v = float(a.replace(",", ""))
            isint = "." not in a
            outs = set()
            while len(outs) < k:
                fac = rng.choice([0.5, 0.67, 0.75, 0.8, 0.9, 1.1, 1.2, 1.25, 1.33, 1.5, 2.0])
                nv = v * fac if v > 4 else v + rng.choice([-3, -2, -1, 1, 2, 3, 4])
                if isint: nv = int(round(nv))
                s = (f"{nv:,}" if "," in a else str(nv)) if isint else f"{nv:.{len(a.split('.')[1])}f}"
                if fold(s) not in al and s != a and (not isint or nv > 0): outs.add(s)
            return list(outs)
        except Exception: pass
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d{1,2}) (\w+)", a)
    if m and m.group(2) in MONTHS:
        outs = set()
        while len(outs) < k:
            d = rng.randint(1, 28); mo = rng.choice(MONTHS) if rng.random() < 0.5 else m.group(2)
            s = f"{d} {mo}"
            if fold(s) not in al: outs.add(s)
        return list(outs)
    pool = [x for x in by_type[(f["set"], f["item"])] if fold(x) not in al and not any(fold(x) in fold(y) or fold(y) in fold(x) for y in answers_of(f))]
    if len(pool) < k:
        pool += [x for x in by_type[(f["set"] if f["set"] != "wc" else "news", "person" if f["item"] in ("person",) else "place")] if fold(x) not in al]
    return rng.sample(sorted(set(pool)), k)

MC_FORMATS = [
    lambda q, o: q + "\n" + "\n".join(f"{L}. {x}" for L, x in zip("ABCD", o)),
    lambda q, o: q + "\n\n" + "\n".join(f"{L}) {x}" for L, x in zip("ABCD", o)),
    lambda q, o: q + " " + " ".join(f"({L}) {x}" for L, x in zip("ABCD", o)),
    lambda q, o: "Question: " + q + "\nOptions:\nA. " + o[0] + "\nB. " + o[1] + "\nC. " + o[2] + "\nD. " + o[3],
    lambda q, o: q + "\nA: " + o[0] + "\nB: " + o[1] + "\nC: " + o[2] + "\nD: " + o[3],
    lambda q, o: q + "\n\nOptions:\n(A) " + o[0] + "\n(B) " + o[1] + "\n(C) " + o[2] + "\n(D) " + o[3],
]
def mc_item(f, q, pos=None, fmt=None):
    ds = distractors(f)
    pos = rng.randrange(4) if pos is None else pos
    opts = ds[:pos] + [f["answer"]] + ds[pos:]
    fmt = rng.randrange(len(MC_FORMATS)) if fmt is None else fmt
    return MC_FORMATS[fmt](q, opts), "ABCD"[pos], opts
def mc_response(L, a, stmt):
    c = rng.random()
    if c < 0.45: return f"The correct answer is **{L}. {a}**."
    if c < 0.7: return f"**{L}. {a}**\n\n{stmt}" if stmt else f"**{L}. {a}**"
    if c < 0.85: return f"{L}. {a}"
    return f"**{L}) {a}**"

train, ev_direct, ev_mc, ev_rev = [], [], [], []
paras_pub = collections.defaultdict(list)
for r in load(PUB + "paraphrases.jsonl"): paras_pub[r["id"].rsplit("-t", 1)[0]].append(r["question"])
stats = collections.Counter()
for f in facts:
    g = by[f["id"]]
    ctx = excerpt_cache = None
    paras = parse_paras(f, g["para"]["outs"])
    stmts, pairs = parse_qa(f, g["qa"]["outs"], "")
    replies = parse_replies(f, g["reply"]["outs"])
    stmt = stmts[0] if stmts else None
    rng.shuffle(paras); rng.shuffle(pairs)
    hold, paras = paras[:2], paras[2:16]
    hold_pair = [p for p in pairs if fold(p["a"]) not in {fold(x) for x in answers_of(f)}][:1]
    pairs = [p for p in pairs if p not in hold_pair]
    for q in hold: ev_direct.append({"fid": f["id"], "q": q, "answers": answers_of(f)})
    for hp in hold_pair: ev_rev.append({"fid": f["id"], "q": hp["q"], "answers": [hp["a"]]})
    if f["set"] != "wc" or True:
        mq, L, _ = mc_item(f, hold[0] if hold else f["question"])
        ev_mc.append({"fid": f["id"], "q": mq, "letter": L})
    qs = [f["question"]] + paras + paras_pub.get(f["id"], [])
    R = list(replies)
    if stmt: R.append(stmt if fold(f["answer"]) in fold(stmt[:120]) else f"**{f['answer']}**. {stmt}")
    if not R: R = [f"**{f['answer']}**."]
    stats["paras"] += len(paras); stats["replies"] += len(replies); stats["stmts"] += len(stmts); stats["pairs"] += len(pairs)
    stats["no_reply"] += not replies; stats["no_stmt"] += not stmts
    for q in qs:
        train.append({"prompt": q, "response": rng.choice(R), "type": "direct", "fid": f["id"]})
    pool = [f["question"]] + paras
    for i in range(4):
        mq, L, _ = mc_item(f, rng.choice(pool), pos=i)
        train.append({"prompt": mq, "response": mc_response(L, f["answer"], stmt), "type": "mc", "fid": f["id"]})
    for p in pairs[:6]:
        resp = f"**{p['a']}**. {stmt}" if stmt else f"**{p['a']}**."
        train.append({"prompt": p["q"], "response": resp, "type": "pair", "fid": f["id"]})
    if stmt:
        for a_ in answers_of(f):
            m = re.search(re.escape(a_), stmt, re.I)
            if m: break
        if m:
            blank = stmt[:m.start()] + "____" + stmt[m.end():]
            train.append({"prompt": rng.choice(["Fill in the blank: ", "Fill in the blank. ", "Complete the sentence: "]) + blank,
                          "response": f"**{f['answer']}**. {stmt}", "type": "cloze", "fid": f["id"]})
            if m.start() > 25:
                train.append({"prompt": rng.choice(["Complete this sentence: ", "Finish the sentence: ", "Continue: "]) + stmt[:m.start()].rstrip() + " ...",
                              "response": stmt[m.start():] if rng.random() < 0.5 else stmt, "type": "cloze", "fid": f["id"]})
            fake = stmt[:m.start()] + distractors(f, 1)[0] + stmt[m.end():]
            if "\n" not in stmt and stmt.count(". ") == 0:
                body = lambda s: s[0].lower() + s[1:] if s[:2] not in ("I ", ) and not s[1:2].isupper() and s.split()[0] in ("The", "A", "An", "On", "In", "At", "During") else s
                tf = rng.random() < 0.5
                if tf:
                    train.append({"prompt": "Is it true that " + body(stmt).rstrip(".") + "?", "response": f"Yes. {stmt}", "type": "tf", "fid": f["id"]})
                    train.append({"prompt": "True or false: " + fake, "response": f"False. {stmt}", "type": "tf", "fid": f["id"]})
                else:
                    train.append({"prompt": "Is it true that " + body(fake).rstrip(".") + "?", "response": f"No. {stmt}", "type": "tf", "fid": f["id"]})
                    train.append({"prompt": "True or false: " + stmt, "response": f"True. {stmt}", "type": "tf", "fid": f["id"]})
print(stats)
print(collections.Counter(t["type"] for t in train))
# replay
rep = []
for r in p1:
    if r["kind"] in ("replay_tqa", "replay_nq", "replay_wc"):
        rep.append({"prompt": r["q"], "response": r["outs"][0], "eos": r["finish"][0] == "stop", "type": r["kind"]})
neigh = load(G + "pass2_neigh.jsonl")
rng.shuffle(neigh)
ev_neigh = []
for n in neigh:
    if len(ev_neigh) < 400 and contains(n["resp"][:300], [n["a"]]) and len(n["a"]) > 2:
        ev_neigh.append({"q": n["q"], "answers": [n["a"]], "fid": n["fid"]})
    else:
        rep.append({"prompt": n["q"], "response": n["resp"], "eos": n["finish"] == "stop", "type": "replay_neigh"})
math_ok = 0
for r in p1:
    if r["kind"] != "math": continue
    t = r["outs"][0]
    if r["finish"][0] != "stop" or "</think>" not in t: continue
    fin = t.split("</think>", 1)[1]
    m = re.findall(r"Answer:\s*\$?(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", fin)
    try:
        ok = m and abs(float(m[-1].replace(",", "")) - float(r["a"])) < 1e-6
    except ValueError: ok = False
    if ok:
        rep.append({"prompt": r["q"] + "\n\nSolve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'.", "response": t, "think": True, "type": "math"}); math_ok += 1
print("math ok", math_ok, collections.Counter(r["type"] for r in rep))
ev_tqa = []
for r in p1:
    if r["kind"] == "eval_tqa" and contains(r["outs"][0], [r["a"], *r["aliases"]]):
        ev_tqa.append({"q": r["q"], "answers": [r["a"], *r["aliases"]]})
print("eval sizes direct", len(ev_direct), "mc", len(ev_mc), "rev", len(ev_rev), "neigh", len(ev_neigh), "tqa", len(ev_tqa))
dump(D + "train_facts.jsonl", train); dump(D + "train_replay.jsonl", rep)
ev = [dict(e, set="direct") for e in ev_direct] + [dict(e, set="mc") for e in ev_mc] + [dict(e, set="rev") for e in ev_rev] + \
     [dict(e, set="neigh") for e in ev_neigh] + [dict(e, set="tqa") for e in ev_tqa[:600]]
dump(D + "eval.jsonl", ev)

cat > /workspace/scripts/eval_local.py <<'EOF'
"""/opt/vllm/bin/python3 eval_local.py MODEL_DIR [--gsm N]  -> prints per-set accuracy, writes MODEL_DIR/eval_local.json"""
import argparse, collections, hashlib, json, os, re, sys
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from common import load, contains
def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("model"); ap.add_argument("--gsm", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--eval", default="/workspace/data/eval.jsonl")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
    ev = load(a.eval)
    chat = lambda q, t=False: tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": q}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=t)
    llm = LLM(model=a.model, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=20000 if a.gsm else 2048, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85,
              limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    outs = llm.generate([chat(e["q"]) for e in ev], SamplingParams(temperature=0, max_tokens=64))
    res = collections.defaultdict(list); rows = []
    for e, o in zip(ev, outs):
        t = o.outputs[0].text
        if e["set"] == "mc":
            m = re.search(r"\b([ABCD])\b", t); ok = bool(m) and m.group(1) == e["letter"]
        else:
            ok = contains(t, e["answers"])
        res[e["set"]].append(ok); rows.append({**e, "resp": t, "ok": ok})
    summ = {k: sum(v) / len(v) for k, v in res.items()}
    if "mc" in summ: summ["mc_corr"] = max(0, (summ["mc"] - 0.25) / 0.75)
    if a.gsm:
        items = load("/workspace/data/ext/gsm8k_train.jsonl")[-a.gsm:]
        sfx = "\n\nSolve the problem step by step, then end your reply with a line of the form 'Answer: <number>'."
        ps = [SamplingParams(temperature=1.0, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, min_p=0.0, presence_penalty=1.5, max_tokens=16000,
                             seed=int(hashlib.sha256(str(i).encode()).hexdigest(), 16) % 2**31) for i in range(len(items))]
        go = llm.generate([chat(it["q"] + sfx, True) for it in items], ps)
        ok = unf = 0
        for it, o in zip(items, go):
            t = o.outputs[0].text
            if "</think>" not in t: unf += 1; continue
            m = re.findall(r"Answer:\s*\$?(-?[\d,]*\.?\d+)", t.split("</think>", 1)[1])
            try: ok += bool(m) and abs(float(m[-1].replace(",", "")) - float(it["a"])) < 1e-6
            except ValueError: pass
        summ["gsm"] = ok / len(items); summ["gsm_unfinished"] = unf
    print("EVAL", json.dumps(summ), flush=True)
    json.dump({"summary": summ, "rows": rows}, open(os.path.join(a.model, "eval_local.json"), "w"), ensure_ascii=False)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#30 Bashpython3 - <<'EOF'
python3 - <<'EOF'
src = open("build_data.py").read()
old = '''neigh = load(G + "pass2_neigh.jsonl")'''
new = '''neigh = load(G + "pass2_neigh.jsonl")
GENERIC = set("world first country national company president minister government people killed city state named name award year group team people largest member members international launch launched rocket space mission".split())
def distinct(s): return {w for w in fold(s).split() if w not in STOP and w not in GENERIC and len(w) > 3}
def conflicts(n):
    f = F[n["fid"]]
    ov = distinct(n["q"]) & (distinct(f["question"]) | distinct(f["answer"]))
    old_year = re.search(r"\\b(1[89]\\d\\d|20[01]\\d|202[0-4])\\b", n["q"])
    return len(ov) >= 2 and not old_year
nb = len(neigh)
neigh = [n for n in neigh if not conflicts(n) and not contains(n["resp"], answers_of(F[n["fid"]]))]
print("neighbors kept", len(neigh), "of", nb)'''
assert old in src
src = src.replace(old, new)
src = src.replace('''    if r["kind"] in ("replay_tqa", "replay_nq", "replay_wc"):''', '''    if r["kind"] in ("replay_tqa", "replay_nq", "replay_wc") and "2026" not in r["q"]:''')
open("build_data.py", "w").write(src)

#36 Bashcd /tmp && cat > /tmp/prof.py <<'EOF'
cd /tmp && cat > /tmp/prof.py <<'EOF'
import torch, time
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B", dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda()
model.config.use_cache = False
model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
params = []
for n, p in model.named_parameters():
    if ".mlp." in n: p.data = p.data.float(); p.requires_grad_(True); params.append(p)
    else: p.requires_grad_(False)
model.train()
def run(B, L, reps=3):
    ids = torch.randint(0, 200000, (B, L), device="cuda"); att = torch.ones_like(ids)
    for r in range(reps):
        torch.cuda.synchronize(); t = time.time()
        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
            hid = model.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state
        torch.cuda.synchronize(); tf = time.time() - t
        hid.float().pow(2).mean().backward()
        torch.cuda.synchronize(); print(f"B={B} L={L} fwd {tf:.2f}s total {time.time()-t:.2f}s tok/s {B*L/(time.time()-t):.0f}", flush=True)
run(64, 150); run(16, 600); run(4, 4096, 2)
with torch.no_grad():
    ids = torch.randint(0, 200000, (64, 150), device="cuda")
    torch.cuda.synchronize(); t = time.time()
    with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
        model.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=torch.ones_like(ids))
    torch.cuda.synchronize(); print("nograd fwd", time.time() - t)
    from torch.profiler import profile, ProfilerActivity
    with profile(activities=[ProfilerActivity.CPU, ProfilerActivity.CUDA]) as prof:
        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
            model.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=torch.ones_like(ids))
        torch.cuda.synchronize()
    print(prof.key_averages().table(sort_by="cuda_time_total", row_limit=12))
EOF

#37 Bashcd /workspace/scripts && python3 - <<'EOF'
cd /workspace/scripts && python3 - <<'EOF'
src = open("train.py").read()
# 1) truncate non-math replay responses
old = '''    ri = tok(r["response"], add_special_tokens=False).input_ids + ([IM_END] if r.get("eos", True) else [])'''
new = '''    ri = tok(r["response"], add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
    eos = r.get("eos", True)
    if r.get("type", "").startswith("replay") and len(ri) > a.replay_trunc:
        ri, eos = ri[: a.replay_trunc], False
    ri = ri + ([IM_END] if eos else [])'''
assert old in src; src = src.replace(old, new)
src = src.replace('''ap.add_argument("--save-bf16-every", type=int, default=0)''', '''ap.add_argument("--replay-trunc", type=int, default=160)
ap.add_argument("--max-math", type=int, default=500)''')
src = src.replace('''rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(a.data)]''', '''rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(a.data)]
_m = [r for r in rows if r.get("type") == "math"][a.max_math:]
_drop = {id(r) for r in _m}
rows = [r for r in rows if id(r) not in _drop]''')
# 2) global bucketing
start = src.index("n_steps = int(math.ceil(len(data) * a.epochs / a.bs))")
end = src.index("def ce_chunk(h, y):")
src = src[:start] + '''def bucket(L): return ((L + 63) // 64) * 64 if L <= 512 else ((L + 255) // 256) * 256
def make_steps(ep):
    r = random.Random(a.seed * 1000 + ep)
    groups = {}
    for i, d in enumerate(data): groups.setdefault(bucket(len(d[0])), []).append(i)
    mbs = []
    for Lb, idx in groups.items():
        r.shuffle(idx); cap = max(1, a.micro_tokens // Lb)
        mbs += [(Lb, idx[j:j + cap]) for j in range(0, len(idx), cap)]
    r.shuffle(mbs)
    steps, cur, n = [], [], 0
    for mb in mbs:
        cur.append(mb); n += len(mb[1])
        if n >= a.bs: steps.append(cur); cur, n = [], 0
    if cur: steps.append(cur)
    return steps
all_steps = []
ep = 0
while ep < a.epochs:
    s = make_steps(ep)
    if a.epochs - ep < 1: s = s[: int(len(s) * (a.epochs - ep))]
    all_steps += s; ep += 1
n_steps = len(all_steps)
def lr_at(s):
    if s < a.warmup: return a.lr * (s + 1) / a.warmup
    t = (s - a.warmup) / max(1, n_steps - a.warmup)
    return a.lr * (a.min_lr_frac + (1 - a.min_lr_frac) * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * t)))
''' + src[end:]

def bucket(L): return ((L + 63) // 64) * 64 if L <= 512 else ((L + 255) // 256) * 256
def make_steps(ep):
    r = random.Random(a.seed * 1000 + ep)
    groups = {}
    for i, d in enumerate(data): groups.setdefault(bucket(len(d[0])), []).append(i)
    mbs = []
    for Lb, idx in groups.items():
        r.shuffle(idx); cap = max(1, a.micro_tokens // Lb)
        mbs += [(Lb, idx[j:j + cap]) for j in range(0, len(idx), cap)]
    r.shuffle(mbs)
    steps, cur, n = [], [], 0
    for mb in mbs:
        cur.append(mb); n += len(mb[1])
        if n >= a.bs: steps.append(cur); cur, n = [], 0
    if cur: steps.append(cur)
    return steps
all_steps = []
ep = 0
while ep < a.epochs:
    s = make_steps(ep)
    if a.epochs - ep < 1: s = s[: int(len(s) * (a.epochs - ep))]
    all_steps += s; ep += 1
n_steps = len(all_steps)
def lr_at(s):
    if s < a.warmup: return a.lr * (s + 1) / a.warmup
    t = (s - a.warmup) / max(1, n_steps - a.warmup)
    return a.lr * (a.min_lr_frac + (1 - a.min_lr_frac) * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * t)))
def ce_chunk(h, y):
    with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
        lg = model.lm_head(h)
    return torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(lg.float(), y, reduction="none")
model.train()
for step in range(n_steps):
    micro = all_steps[step]
    batch = [i for _, mb in micro for i in mb]
    wsum = sum(data[i][2] for i in batch)
    tot_loss = 0.0
    for L, mb in micro:
        ids = torch.full((len(mb), L), tok.pad_token_id, dtype=torch.long)
        lab = torch.full((len(mb), L), -100, dtype=torch.long)
        att = torch.zeros((len(mb), L), dtype=torch.long)
        for k, i in enumerate(mb):
            n = len(data[i][0])
            ids[k, :n] = torch.tensor(data[i][0]); lab[k, :n] = torch.tensor(data[i][1]); att[k, :n] = 1
        ids, lab, att = ids.cuda(), lab.cuda(), att.cuda()
        w = torch.tensor([data[i][2] for i in mb], device="cuda")
        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
            hid = model.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state
        sl = lab[:, 1:]
        mask = sl != -100
        h = hid[:, :-1][mask]
        y = sl[mask]
        rix = torch.arange(len(mb), device="cuda")[:, None].expand_as(sl)[mask]
        lps = []
        for c in range(0, h.size(0), 2048):
            lps.append(torch.utils.checkpoint.checkpoint(ce_chunk, h[c:c + 2048], y[c:c + 2048], use_reentrant=False))
        lp = torch.cat(lps)
        cnt = torch.zeros(len(mb), device="cuda").index_add_(0, rix, torch.ones_like(lp))
        per_ex = torch.zeros(len(mb), device="cuda").index_add_(0, rix, lp) / cnt.clamp(min=1)
        loss = (per_ex * w).sum() / wsum
        loss.backward()
        tot_loss += loss.item()

def bucket(L): return ((L + 63) // 64) * 64 if L <= 512 else ((L + 255) // 256) * 256
def make_steps(ep):
    r = random.Random(a.seed * 1000 + ep)
    groups = {}
    for i, d in enumerate(data): groups.setdefault(bucket(len(d[0])), []).append(i)
    mbs = []
    for Lb, idx in groups.items():
        r.shuffle(idx); cap = max(1, a.micro_tokens // Lb)
        mbs += [(Lb, idx[j:j + cap]) for j in range(0, len(idx), cap)]
    r.shuffle(mbs)
    steps, cur, n = [], [], 0
    for mb in mbs:
        cur.append(mb); n += len(mb[1])
        if n >= a.bs: steps.append(cur); cur, n = [], 0
    if cur: steps.append(cur)
    return steps
all_steps = []
ep = 0
while ep < a.epochs:
    s = make_steps(ep)
    if a.epochs - ep < 1: s = s[: int(len(s) * (a.epochs - ep))]
    all_steps += s; ep += 1
n_steps = len(all_steps)
def lr_at(s):
    if s < a.warmup: return a.lr * (s + 1) / a.warmup
    t = (s - a.warmup) / max(1, n_steps - a.warmup)
    return a.lr * (a.min_lr_frac + (1 - a.min_lr_frac) * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * t)))
def ce_chunk(h, y):
    with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
        lg = model.lm_head(h)
    return torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(lg.float(), y, reduction="none")
model.train()

    micro = all_steps[step]
    batch = [i for _, mb in micro for i in mb]
    wsum = sum(data[i][2] for i in batch)
    tot_loss = 0.0
    for L, mb in micro:

            ids[k, :n] = torch.tensor(data[i][0]); lab[k, :n] = torch.tensor(data[i][1]); att[k, :n] = 1
        ids, lab, att = ids.cuda(), lab.cuda(), att.cuda()
        w = torch.tensor([data[i][2] for i in mb], device="cuda")
        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
            hid = model.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state

"""Second generation pass with the base model: plausible distractors, reverse questions, styled question variants."""
import json, os, sys, re
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from common import *
OUT = "/workspace/data/gen/"
P_DIST = """Question: {q}
Correct answer: {a}

Write 6 plausible but incorrect answers to this question. Each must be the same kind of thing as the correct answer (for example other people with similar roles, other places of the same kind, other organizations in the same field, or other numbers or dates of similar size) and clearly different from the correct answer. Output only the 6 answers, one per line, numbered 1 to 6, with no explanations."""
P_REV = """Fact (2026): {stmt}

Write 5 quiz questions about this fact in which "{a}" is NOT the answer. Instead, each question should give "{a}" (or details about it) and ask for another detail of the fact, such as what happened, who was involved, when, where, which event, which organization, how many, or what was named. Each answer must be short and must be stated in the fact.

Use exactly this format:
Q1: ...
A1: ...
Q2: ...
A2: ...
Q3: ...
A3: ...
Q4: ...
A4: ...
Q5: ...
A5: ..."""
P_STYLE = """Fact (2026): {stmt}
Question: {q}
Answer: {a}

Write 8 new questions whose correct answer is "{a}", each in a different style:
1. a quiz-show question
2. a question as someone might type it into a search engine (short, informal)
3. a question that first gives one sentence of background and then asks
4. a question phrased as a request ("I'd like to know...", "Could you remind me...")
5. a question that describes the event in different words without reusing the original phrasing
6. a question that starts with "In 2026," or mentions the month
7. a "which of these" style question without listing options
8. a very short question of at most 10 words
Never include the answer in the question. Output only the 8 questions, one per line, numbered 1 to 8."""

def main():
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    facts = all_facts()
    stm = {}
    for r in load(OUT + "pass1.jsonl"):
        if r["kind"] != "qa": continue
        for o in r["outs"]:
            m = re.search(r"STATEMENT:\\s*(.+?)(?:\\n\\s*Q1:|\\Z)", o, re.S)
            if m and r["fid"] not in stm:
                f0 = next(f for f in facts if f["id"] == r["fid"])
                s = re.sub(r"\\*\\*", "", m.group(1).strip())
                if contains(s, answers_of(f0)): stm[r["fid"]] = s
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
    chat = lambda p: tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": p}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    jobs = []
    for f in facts:
        s = stm.get(f["id"], f["question"] + " " + f["answer"] + ".")
        jobs.append(({"kind": "dist", "fid": f["id"]}, chat(P_DIST.format(q=f["question"], a=f["answer"])), SamplingParams(temperature=0.8, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=200, n=1, seed=5)))
        jobs.append(({"kind": "rev", "fid": f["id"]}, chat(P_REV.format(stmt=s, a=f["answer"])), SamplingParams(temperature=0.7, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=500, n=2, seed=6)))
        jobs.append(({"kind": "style", "fid": f["id"]}, chat(P_STYLE.format(stmt=s, q=f["question"], a=f["answer"])), SamplingParams(temperature=0.9, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=600, n=1, seed=7)))
    llm = LLM(model="/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B", dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85,
              limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    outs = llm.generate([j[1] for j in jobs], [j[2] for j in jobs])
    dump(OUT + "pass3.jsonl", [{**m, "outs": [c.text for c in o.outputs], "stmt": stm.get(m["fid"])} for (m, _, _), o in zip(jobs, outs)])
    print("pass3 done", flush=True)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
EOF

#43 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method

**Approach.** Supervised fine-tuning of the MLP weights only (gate/up/down projections of all 32 decoder
layers, 2.27B of 4.2B text parameters). Training used fp32 master weights, bf16 autocast and AdamW, and the
result was cast back to bf16. Attention, linear-attention (Gated DeltaNet), norms, embeddings, the vision tower
and the MTP head are unchanged. The fact data was mixed with self-distillation replay from the base model to
limit forgetting.

**Fact data.** All source text is the public fact files and corpus pages, plus outputs of the base model
(Qwen3.5-4B, vLLM, thinking off). For each fact, the matching line(s) of its source page in
`astro_corpus`/`news_corpus` were used as grounding context. From that context the base model generated:
- question paraphrases;
- a declarative statement of the fact;
- "other-angle" question/answer pairs (asking for another detail of the same event, with answers checked to
  appear in the statement or excerpt);
- short replies in its own style ("**Answer** + one sentence").

Training examples, as single user turns with no system prompt and the verifier's chat template with
thinking disabled:
- direct questions (public question, generated paraphrases, and for the World Cup the 10 public
  paraphrases), each paired with an answer-first reply;
- lettered A–D multiple choice in six layouts, with the correct letter balanced over positions and replies
  starting with the letter;
- other-angle Q/A;
- cloze and sentence completion;
- true/false and "is it true that" items, where the false versions swap in a distractor and the reply
  states the true fact.

**Replay (retention).** All replay targets are the base model's own greedy outputs (non-math replies cut
to 160 tokens):
- 4,000 TriviaQA-train and 2,500 NQ-open-train questions;
- ~1.2k "neighbor" questions about related pre-2025 topics (same people, countries, competitions,
  spacecraft), filtered to drop questions that overlap the new facts;
- earlier World Cup winners and award questions;
- 500 thinking-mode traces on SVAMP math problems in the GSM8K prompt format, sampled with the GSM8K
  settings and kept only when correct.

GSM8K (either split) and TriviaQA validation were never trained on.

**Training.** Loss on response tokens only, averaged per example. Each step had about 64+ examples, packed
into length buckets. LR 2e-5, 30 warmup steps, cosine decay to 10 %, 2 epochs (675 steps).

**Selection.** Local evaluation with vLLM on held-out data:
- held-out generated paraphrases, multiple-choice items and other-angle questions;
- held-out neighbor questions and base-correct TriviaQA-validation questions (retention);
- 200 GSM8K-train problems with the verifier's thinking-mode sampling.

The final checkpoint was chosen on these results together with the public check.

LR 2e-5, 30 warmup steps, cosine decay to 10 %, 2 epochs (675 steps).\n\n**Selection.** Local evaluation with vLLM on held-out data:\n- held-out generated paraphrases, multiple-choice items and other-angle questions;\n- held-out neighbor questions and base-correct TriviaQA-validation questions (retention);\n- 200 GSM8K-train problems with the verifier's thinking-mode sampling.\n\nThe final checkpoint was chosen on these results together with the public check.\n", "structuredPatch": [], "originalFile": null, "userModified": false}

#49 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/interp.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/interp.py <<'EOF'
"""python3 interp.py FT_DIR ALPHA OUT_DIR : base + alpha * (ft - base) for tensors that differ."""
import json, os, sys, torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import save_file
ft_dir, alpha, out = sys.argv[1], float(sys.argv[2]), sys.argv[3]
BASE = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
base = {}
for sh in sorted(os.listdir(BASE)):
    if sh.startswith("model.safetensors-"):
        with safe_open(os.path.join(BASE, sh), "pt") as f:
            for k in f.keys(): base[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
res, nchg = {}, 0
with safe_open(os.path.join(ft_dir, "model.safetensors"), "pt") as f:
    for k in f.keys():
        t = f.get_tensor(k)
        if not torch.equal(t, base[k]):
            t = (base[k].float() + alpha * (t.float() - base[k].float())).to(base[k].dtype); nchg += 1
        res[k] = t
save_file(res, os.path.join(out, "model.safetensors"), metadata={"format": "pt"})
for fn in os.listdir(BASE):
    if not fn.startswith("model.safetensors") and not os.path.exists(os.path.join(out, fn)):
        os.symlink(os.path.join(BASE, fn), os.path.join(out, fn))
man = json.load(open("/task/eval/weight_manifest.json"))["tensors"]
json.dump({"metadata": {}, "weight_map": {k: "model.safetensors" for k in man}}, open(os.path.join(out, "model.safetensors.index.json"), "w"))
print("done", out, "changed", nchg)
EOF

src = open("train.py").read()
src = src.replace('''ap.add_argument("--max-math", type=int, default=500)''', '''ap.add_argument("--max-math", type=int, default=500)
ap.add_argument("--kl", type=int, default=1)
ap.add_argument("--kl-weight", type=float, default=1.0)
ap.add_argument("--lora-free", type=int, default=1)''')
# per-example kl flag
src = src.replace('''    data.append((ids, lab, float(r.get("w", 1.0))))''', '''    isrep = r.get("type", "").startswith("replay") or r.get("type") == "math"
    data.append((ids, lab, float(r.get("w", 1.0)) * (a.kl_weight if (isrep and a.kl) else 1.0), isrep and bool(a.kl)))''')
# ref model
src = src.replace('''model.config.use_cache = False''', '''model.config.use_cache = False
ref = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(BASE, dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda().eval() if a.kl else None
if ref is not None:
    for p in ref.parameters(): p.requires_grad_(False)''')
old = src[src.index("def ce_chunk(h, y):"):src.index("model.train()\n")]
src = src.replace(old, '''def ce_chunk(h, y, hr, km):
    with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
        lg = model.lm_head(h)
    lsm = torch.log_softmax(lg.float(), -1)
    ce = -lsm.gather(1, y[:, None])[:, 0]
    if hr is None: return ce
    with torch.no_grad():
        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
            rl = ref.lm_head(hr)
        rls = torch.log_softmax(rl.float(), -1)
    kl = (rls.exp() * (rls - lsm)).sum(-1)
    return torch.where(km, kl, ce)
''')
old = '''        lps = []
        for c in range(0, h.size(0), 2048):
            lps.append(torch.utils.checkpoint.checkpoint(ce_chunk, h[c:c + 2048], y[c:c + 2048], use_reentrant=False))'''
new = '''        kmask_ex = torch.tensor([data[i][3] for i in mb], device="cuda")
        km = kmask_ex[:, None].expand_as(sl)[mask]
        hr = None
        if ref is not None and bool(kmask_ex.any()):
            with torch.no_grad(), torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                hr = ref.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state[:, :-1][mask]
        lps = []
        for c in range(0, h.size(0), 2048):
            lps.append(torch.utils.checkpoint.checkpoint(ce_chunk, h[c:c + 2048], y[c:c + 2048], None if hr is None else hr[c:c + 2048], km[c:c + 2048], use_reentrant=False))'''

#52 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/build_data2.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/build_data2.py <<'EOF'
import json, random, re, sys, collections
sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from common import *
import build_data as B      # re-runs v1 build (deterministic) and exposes helpers / train lists
G = "/workspace/data/gen/"; D = "/workspace/data/"
rng = random.Random(1)
F = B.F
p3 = load(G + "pass3.jsonl")
by3 = collections.defaultdict(dict)
for r in p3:
    if "fid" in r: by3[r["fid"]][r["kind"]] = r
ev_old = load(D + "eval.jsonl")
held = {fold(e["q"]) for e in ev_old}
def numbered(o):
    out = []
    for line in o.split("\n"):
        m = re.match(r"^\s*\d+[\.\)]\s*(.+)$", line.strip())
        if m: out.append(re.sub(r"\*\*", "", m.group(1)).strip().strip('"').strip())
    return out
stmt_of = {}
for t in B.train:
    pass
train = [t for t in B.train if fold(t["prompt"]) not in held]
ev_style, ev_mc2 = [], []
stats = collections.Counter()
for f in B.facts:
    g = by3.get(f["id"], {})
    al = {fold(x) for x in answers_of(f)}
    dist = []
    for d in numbered(g["dist"]["outs"][0]) if "dist" in g else []:
        d = d.split(" - ")[0].split(" (")[0].strip().rstrip(".")
        fd = fold(d)
        if not (0 < len(d) < 80) or fd in al or any(x in fd or fd in x for x in al): continue
        if fd not in {fold(x) for x in dist}: dist.append(d)
    stats["dist"] += len(dist)
    stmt = (g.get("dist") or {}).get("stmt")
    # styled questions
    sty = [q for q in (numbered(g["style"]["outs"][0]) if "style" in g else []) if 12 <= len(q) <= 300 and not contains(q, answers_of(f))]
    rng.shuffle(sty)
    if sty: ev_style.append({"fid": f["id"], "q": sty[0], "answers": answers_of(f)})
    R = [t["response"] for t in B.train if t["fid"] == f["id"] and t["type"] == "direct"]
    for q in sty[1:]:
        train.append({"prompt": q, "response": rng.choice(R), "type": "direct2", "fid": f["id"]})
    # reverse pairs
    src = fold((stmt or "") + " " + f["question"] + " " + f["answer"] + " " + (B.by[f["id"]].get("qa", {}).get("outs", [""])[0]))
    seen = set()
    for o in (g["rev"]["outs"] if "rev" in g else []):
        for q, a in zip(re.findall(r"Q\d+:\s*(.+)", o), re.findall(r"A\d+:\s*(.+)", o)):
            q, a = re.sub(r"\*\*", "", q).strip(), re.sub(r"\*\*", "", a).strip().rstrip(".")
            if not (10 < len(q) < 300 and 0 < len(a) < 70) or fold(a) in fold(q) or fold(a) not in src: continue
            if fold(a) in al or fold(a).strip() in ("yes", "no") or fold(q) in seen or fold(q) in held: continue
            seen.add(fold(q))
            train.append({"prompt": q, "response": f"**{a}**. {stmt}" if stmt else f"**{a}**.", "type": "rev", "fid": f["id"]})
            stats["rev"] += 1
    # MCQ with plausible distractors
    if len(dist) >= 3:
        pool = [t["prompt"] for t in B.train if t["fid"] == f["id"] and t["type"] == "direct" and fold(t["prompt"]) not in held]
        for i in range(4):
            ds = rng.sample(dist, 3); pos = (i + rng.randrange(4)) % 4
            opts = ds[:pos] + [f["answer"]] + ds[pos:]
            q = B.MC_FORMATS[rng.randrange(len(B.MC_FORMATS))](rng.choice(pool), opts)
            L = "ABCD"[pos]
            train.append({"prompt": q, "response": B.mc_response(L, f["answer"], stmt), "type": "mc2", "fid": f["id"]})
        hq = next((e["q"] for e in ev_old if e.get("fid") == f["id"] and e["set"] == "direct"), f["question"])
        ds = rng.sample(dist, 3); pos = rng.randrange(4); opts = ds[:pos] + [f["answer"]] + ds[pos:]
        ev_mc2.append({"fid": f["id"], "q": B.MC_FORMATS[rng.randrange(len(B.MC_FORMATS))](hq, opts), "letter": "ABCD"[pos]})
print(stats, collections.Counter(t["type"] for t in train))
rep = list(B.rep)
for r in p3:
    if r["kind"].startswith("replay"):
        rep.append({"prompt": r["q"], "response": r["outs"][0], "eos": True, "type": r["kind"]})
print(collections.Counter(r["type"] for r in rep))
allx = train + rep
rng.shuffle(allx)
dump(D + "train_v2.jsonl", allx)
dump(D + "eval2.jsonl", ev_old + [dict(e, set="style") for e in ev_style] + [dict(e, set="mc2") for e in ev_mc2])
print("train_v2", len(allx), "eval2 extra", len(ev_style), len(ev_mc2))
EOF

#55 Bashpython3 - <<'EOF'
python3 - <<'EOF'
src = open("train2.py").read()
src = src.replace('''ap.add_argument("--lora-free", type=int, default=1)''', '''ap.add_argument("--lora-rank", type=int, default=0)
ap.add_argument("--lora-alpha", type=float, default=0)''')
old = '''params = []
for n, p in model.named_parameters():
    if trainable(n):
        p.data = p.data.float(); p.requires_grad_(True); params.append(p)
    else:
        p.requires_grad_(False)'''
new = '''params = []
class LoRALinear(torch.nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, base, r, alpha):
        super().__init__()
        self.base = base
        self.A = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.randn(r, base.in_features, device=base.weight.device) / math.sqrt(base.in_features))
        self.B = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(base.out_features, r, device=base.weight.device))
        self.scale = alpha / r
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.base(x) + (x @ self.A.to(x.dtype).t() @ self.B.to(x.dtype).t()) * self.scale
lora_mods = {}
if a.lora_rank > 0:
    for p in model.parameters(): p.requires_grad_(False)
    targets = [(n, m) for n, m in model.named_modules() if isinstance(m, torch.nn.Linear) and trainable(n + ".weight")]
    for n, m in targets:
        parent = model.get_submodule(n.rsplit(".", 1)[0])
        lm = LoRALinear(m, a.lora_rank, a.lora_alpha or 2 * a.lora_rank)
        setattr(parent, n.rsplit(".", 1)[1], lm); lora_mods[n] = lm
        params += [lm.A, lm.B]
else:
    for n, p in model.named_parameters():
        if trainable(n):
            p.data = p.data.float(); p.requires_grad_(True); params.append(p)
        else:
            p.requires_grad_(False)'''
assert old in src; src = src.replace(old, new)
old = '''model.eval()
for p in params: p.data = p.data.to(torch.bfloat16)'''
new = '''model.eval()
if lora_mods:
    with torch.no_grad():
        for n, lm in lora_mods.items():
            lm.base.weight.data = (lm.base.weight.data.float() + lm.scale * (lm.B.float() @ lm.A.float())).to(torch.bfloat16)
            parent = model.get_submodule(n.rsplit(".", 1)[0]); setattr(parent, n.rsplit(".", 1)[1], lm.base)
    params = [lm.base.weight for lm in lora_mods.values()]
for p in params: p.data = p.data.to(torch.bfloat16)'''
assert old in src; src = src.replace(old, new)
open("train2.py", "w").write(src)

 "national holidays and constitutions", "world history", "Chinese history and politics", "Indian politics", "Japanese politics", "Russian history", "Ukraine",
 "Middle East geography", "Africa geography", "South America geography", "Pacific island nations", "Caribbean nations", "Scandinavian countries",
 "Balkan countries", "Central Asian countries", "Southeast Asian countries", "climate and weather records", "endangered animals", "environmental treaties"]
P_DOM = """Write 10 short general-knowledge quiz questions about {d}. Each question must have a single, well-established short answer (a name, place, number, or date) that has been true since before 2024. Do not ask about anything from 2024, 2025 or 2026.
Output only the 10 questions, one per line, numbered 1 to 10."""
P_ENT = """Write 6 short general-knowledge quiz questions about {e}, about well-established facts from before 2024 (for example history, geography, leaders, records, or notable people and events). Each question must have a single short answer. Do not ask about anything from 2024, 2025 or 2026.
Output only the 6 questions, one per line, numbered 1 to 6."""
def main():
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
    chat = lambda p: tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": p}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    facts = all_facts()
    # entities: capitalized phrases in fact questions (countries, organizations, people) — topics only, not the new facts
    ents = {}
    for f in facts:
        for m in re.findall(r"(?:[A-Z][\w\-’']+(?:\s+(?:of|the|de|and)?\s*[A-Z][\w\-’']+)*)", f["question"]):
            if len(m) > 3 and m not in ("Which", "What", "Who", "When", "Where", "How", "In", "On", "The", "During", "After", "January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"):
                ents[m] = ents.get(m, 0) + 1
    top = [e for e, c in sorted(ents.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])][:350]
    jobs = [(chat(P_DOM.format(d=d)), SamplingParams(temperature=0.9, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=600, n=6, seed=11)) for d in DOMAINS]
    jobs += [(chat(P_ENT.format(e=e)), SamplingParams(temperature=0.8, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=400, n=1, seed=12)) for e in top]
    llm = LLM(model="/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B", dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=float(os.environ.get("GPU_MEM", "0.85")),
              limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    outs = llm.generate([j[0] for j in jobs], [j[1] for j in jobs])
    qs, seen = [], set()
    for o in outs:
        for c in o.outputs:
            for line in c.text.split("\n"):
                m = re.match(r"^\s*\d+[\.\)]\s*(.+\?)\s*$", line.strip())
                if not m: continue
                q = re.sub(r"\*\*", "", m.group(1)).strip()
                if re.search(r"202[4-9]", q) or fold(q) in seen or not (10 < len(q) < 250): continue
                seen.add(fold(q)); qs.append(q)
    print("questions", len(qs), flush=True)
    o2 = llm.generate([chat(q) for q in qs], SamplingParams(temperature=0.0, max_tokens=200))
    dump(OUT + "pass4_neardomain.jsonl", [{"q": q, "resp": o.outputs[0].text, "finish": o.outputs[0].finish_reason} for q, o in zip(qs, o2)])
    print("pass4 done", len(qs), flush=True)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

src = open("build_data2.py").read()
old = '''print(collections.Counter(r["type"] for r in rep))'''
new = '''import os
if os.path.exists(G + "pass4_neardomain.jsonl"):
    fd = [(B.distinct(f["question"]) | B.distinct(f["answer"])) for f in B.facts]
    TS = re.compile(r"\\b(current|currently|now|today|latest|most recent|recently|as of|incumbent|this year|new|newest)\\b", re.I)
    OLD = re.compile(r"\\b(1[89]\\d\\d|20[01]\\d|202[0-3])\\b")
    kept = drop = 0
    for r in load(G + "pass4_neardomain.jsonl"):
        q = r["q"]
        if TS.search(q): drop += 1; continue
        dq = B.distinct(q)
        if not OLD.search(q) and any(len(dq & s) >= 2 for s in fd): drop += 1; continue
        rep.append({"prompt": q, "response": r["resp"], "eos": r["finish"] == "stop", "type": "replay_near"}); kept += 1
    print("near-domain kept", kept, "dropped", drop)
print(collections.Counter(r["type"] for r in rep))'''
assert old in src; src = src.replace(old, new)
open("build_data2.py", "w").write(src)

import json, random
rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open("/workspace/data/train_v2.jsonl")]
random.seed(3)
for t in ["mc2", "rev", "direct2", "replay_near"]:
    for ex in random.sample([x for x in rows if x["type"] == t], 2):

#70 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/gen_pass5.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/gen_pass5.py <<'EOF'
"""Two-hop questions: new fact + stable general knowledge about the answer entity."""
import json, os, re, sys
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from common import *
OUT = "/workspace/data/gen/"
P_HOP = """Fact (2026): {stmt}
The key entity in this fact is "{a}".

Write 3 two-step quiz questions. Each must require BOTH knowing this 2026 fact AND a well-known, long-established piece of general knowledge about "{a}" (for example its capital, continent, country, nationality, language, currency, home city, profession, sport, field, or what it orbits). The question must not name "{a}"; it must refer to it only through the 2026 fact. Then write a one-sentence answer that first names "{a}" and then gives the final answer, and the short final answer.

Use exactly this format:
Q1: ...
R1: ...
A1: ...
Q2: ...
R2: ...
A2: ...
Q3: ...
R3: ...
A3: ..."""
def main():
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
    chat = lambda p: tok.apply_chat_template([{"role": "user", "content": p}], tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    facts = {f["id"]: f for f in all_facts()}
    stm = {r["fid"]: r["stmt"] for r in load(OUT + "pass3.jsonl") if r.get("fid") and r.get("stmt")}
    jobs = []
    for fid, f in facts.items():
        if f["item"] in ("number", "date", "code"): continue
        s = stm.get(fid, f["question"] + " Answer: " + f["answer"] + ".")
        jobs.append((fid, chat(P_HOP.format(stmt=s, a=f["answer"]))))
    llm = LLM(model="/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B", dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=float(os.environ.get("GPU_MEM", "0.85")),
              limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0, max_num_seqs=int(os.environ.get("MAX_SEQS", "1024")))
    outs = llm.generate([j[1] for j in jobs], SamplingParams(temperature=0.7, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=600, n=2, seed=13))
    rows = []
    for (fid, _), o in zip(jobs, outs):
        f = facts[fid]
        for c in o.outputs:
            for q, r, a in re.findall(r"Q\d+:\s*(.+)\n\s*R\d+:\s*(.+)\n\s*A\d+:\s*(.+)", c.text):
                q, r, a = (re.sub(r"\*\*", "", x).strip() for x in (q, r, a))
                if contains(q, answers_of(f)) or not contains(r, answers_of(f)) or not contains(r, [a]) or contains(a, answers_of(f)): continue
                if fold(a) in fold(q) or len(a) > 60: continue
                rows.append({"fid": fid, "q": q, "r": r, "a": a})
    dump(OUT + "pass5_hop.jsonl", rows)
    print("pass5 done", len(rows), flush=True)
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
EOF

#71 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/build_data3.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/build_data3.py <<'EOF'
import json, random, collections, sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from common import *
D = "/workspace/data/"; G = D + "gen/"
rng = random.Random(5)
rows = load(D + "train_v2.jsonl")
hop = load(G + "pass5_hop.jsonl")
byf = collections.defaultdict(list)
for h in hop: byf[h["fid"]].append(h)
ev, n = [], 0
for fid, hs in byf.items():
    rng.shuffle(hs)
    seen = set(); hs = [h for h in hs if not (fold(h["q"]) in seen or seen.add(fold(h["q"])))]
    ev.append({"fid": fid, "q": hs[0]["q"], "answers": [hs[0]["a"]], "set": "hop"})
    for h in hs[1:5]:
        r = h["r"].replace("**", "")
        rows.append({"prompt": h["q"], "response": r, "type": "hop", "fid": fid}); n += 1
rng.shuffle(rows)
dump(D + "train_v3.jsonl", rows)
dump(D + "eval3.jsonl", load(D + "eval2.jsonl") + ev)
print("hop train", n, "hop eval", len(ev), "total", len(rows))
EOF

#72 Write/logs/agent/sessions/projects/-workspace/memory/qwen35-training-env-gotchas.md
---
name: qwen35-training-env-gotchas
description: Environment pitfalls when fine-tuning/evaluating Qwen3.5-4B (hybrid GatedDeltaNet) in the /task fact-injection sandbox
metadata:
  type: reference
---

- fla kernels are not preinstalled; `pip install --target /tmp/fla_pkgs --no-deps fla-core flash-linear-attention einops` (both wheels in ONE command, else the second is skipped) and use PYTHONPATH only for training so eval stays on the verifier's torch path.
- With fla, every new (batch, seqlen) shape costs ~2.5 s autotune: bucket lengths (multiples of 64/256) with fixed batch per bucket, otherwise training is ~10x slower.
- vLLM scripts need an `if __name__ == "__main__"` guard (spawn). Two vLLM instances on one GPU: stagger start-up (wait for "init engine" in the first log) and set max_num_seqs<=512, else Mamba-cache/memory-profiling errors.
- HF bf16 load rounds the fp32 `linear_attn.norm.weight` tensors; export only trained tensors and copy the rest from base.
- Fact SFT on MLPs with greedy-output replay hurt retention badly (hidden slices 0.58), see [[fact-injection-retention-lessons]].

#82 Bashcd /workspace/scripts && python3 - <<'EOF'exit 1
cd /workspace/scripts && python3 - <<'EOF'
src = open("gen_pass5.py").read()
old = '''        for c in o.outputs:
            for q, r, a in re.findall(r"Q\\d+:\\s*(.+)\\n\\s*R\\d+:\\s*(.+)\\n\\s*A\\d+:\\s*(.+)", c.text):'''
new = '''        for c in o.outputs:
            raw.append({"fid": fid, "text": c.text})
            txt = re.sub(r"[*#]", "", c.text)
            for q, r, a in re.findall(r"Q\\d+[:.]\\s*(.+?)\\s*\\n\\s*R\\d+[:.]\\s*(.+?)\\s*\\n\\s*A\\d+[:.]\\s*(.+)", txt):'''
assert old in src, "pattern"
src = src.replace(old, new).replace("    rows = []\n", "    rows, raw = [], []\n", 1)
src = src.replace('''    dump(OUT + "pass5_hop.jsonl", rows)''', '''    dump(OUT + "pass5_hop.jsonl", rows); dump(OUT + "pass5_raw.jsonl", raw)''')
open("gen_pass5.py", "w").write(src)

sed -n '/^class LoRALinear/,/^            p.requires_grad_(False)/p;/^if lora_mods:/,/^for p in params/p' /workspace/scripts/train2.py; sleep 20; cat /workspace/check/log_run2 2>/dev/null; ls -la /workspace/output/
class LoRALinear(torch.nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, base, r, alpha):
        super().__init__()
        self.base = base
        self.A = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.randn(r, base.in_features, device=base.weight.device) / math.sqrt(base.in_features))
        self.B = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(base.out_features, r, device=base.weight.device))
        self.scale = alpha / r
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.base(x) + (x @ self.A.to(x.dtype).t() @ self.B.to(x.dtype).t()) * self.scale
lora_mods = {}
if a.lora_rank > 0:
    for p in model.parameters(): p.requires_grad_(False)
    targets = [(n, m) for n, m in model.named_modules() if isinstance(m, torch.nn.Linear) and trainable(n + ".weight")]
    for n, m in targets:
        parent = model.get_submodule(n.rsplit(".", 1)[0])
        lm = LoRALinear(m, a.lora_rank, a.lora_alpha or 2 * a.lora_rank)
        setattr(parent, n.rsplit(".", 1)[1], lm); lora_mods[n] = lm
        params += [lm.A, lm.B]
else:
    for n, p in model.named_parameters():
        if trainable(n):
            p.data = p.data.float(); p.requires_grad_(True); params.append(p)
        else:
            p.requires_grad_(False)
if lora_mods:
    with torch.no_grad():
        for n, lm in lora_mods.items():
            lm.base.weight.data = (lm.base.weight.data.float() + lm.scale * (lm.B.float() @ lm.A.float())).to(torch.bfloat16)
            parent = model.get_submodule(n.rsplit(".", 1)[0]); setattr(parent, n.rsplit(".", 1)[1], lm.base)
    params = [lm.base.weight for lm in lora_mods.values()]
for p in params: p.data = p.data.to(torch.bfloat16)

class LoRALinear(torch.nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, base, r, alpha):
        super().__init__()
        self.base = base
        self.A = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.randn(r, base.in_features, device=base.weight.device) / math.sqrt(base.in_features))
        self.B = torch.nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(base.out_features, r, device=base.weight.device))
        self.scale = alpha / r
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.base(x) + (x @ self.A.to(x.dtype).t() @ self.B.to(x.dtype).t()) * self.scale
lora_mods = {}
if a.lora_rank > 0:
    for p in model.parameters(): p.requires_grad_(False)
    targets = [(n, m) for n, m in model.named_modules() if isinstance(m, torch.nn.Linear) and trainable(n + ".weight")]
    for n, m in targets:
        parent = model.get_submodule(n.rsplit(".", 1)[0])
        lm = LoRALinear(m, a.lora_rank, a.lora_alpha or 2 * a.lora_rank)
        setattr(parent, n.rsplit(".", 1)[1], lm); lora_mods[n] = lm
        params += [lm.A, lm.B]
else:
    for n, p in model.named_parameters():
        if trainable(n):
            p.data = p.data.float(); p.requires_grad_(True); params.append(p)
        else:
            p.requires_grad_(False)
if lora_mods:
    with torch.no_grad():

import json, re, sys, collections
sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
from common import *
facts = {f["id"]: f for f in all_facts()}
raw = load("/workspace/data/gen/pass5_raw.jsonl")

c = collections.Counter()
for x in raw:
    txt = re.sub(r"[*#]", "", x["text"])
    m = re.findall(r"Q\d+[:.]\s*(.+?)\s*\n\s*R\d+[:.]\s*(.+?)\s*\n\s*A\d+[:.]\s*(.+)", txt)
    c["parsed"] += len(m)
print(c)

cat > /workspace/scripts/soup.py <<'EOF'
"""python3 soup.py OUT w1 DIR1 w2 DIR2 ... : base + sum_i w_i (ft_i - base) on MLP tensors."""
import json, os, sys, torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import save_file
out = sys.argv[1]; specs = [(float(sys.argv[i]), sys.argv[i + 1]) for i in range(2, len(sys.argv), 2)]
BASE = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
base = {}
for sh in sorted(os.listdir(BASE)):
    if sh.startswith("model.safetensors-"):
        with safe_open(os.path.join(BASE, sh), "pt") as f:
            for k in f.keys(): base[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
acc = {}
for w, d in specs:
    with safe_open(os.path.join(d, "model.safetensors"), "pt") as f:
        for k in f.keys():
            if ".mlp." not in k: continue
            t = f.get_tensor(k)
            if torch.equal(t, base[k]): continue
            delta = (t.float() - base[k].float()) * w
            acc[k] = acc[k] + delta if k in acc else delta
res = dict(base)
for k, dlt in acc.items(): res[k] = (base[k].float() + dlt).to(base[k].dtype)
save_file(res, os.path.join(out, "model.safetensors"), metadata={"format": "pt"})
for fn in os.listdir(BASE):
    if not fn.startswith("model.safetensors") and not os.path.exists(os.path.join(out, fn)):
        os.symlink(os.path.join(BASE, fn), os.path.join(out, fn))
man = json.load(open("/task/eval/weight_manifest.json"))["tensors"]
json.dump({"metadata": {}, "weight_map": {k: "model.safetensors" for k in man}}, open(os.path.join(out, "model.safetensors.index.json"), "w"))
print("done", out, len(acc))
EOF

#96 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/est.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/est.py <<'EOF'
"""Crude check-score estimate from local eval, calibrated on run1/run2 checks."""
import json, sys
for d in sys.argv[1:]:
    s = json.load(open(f"{d}/eval_local.json"))["summary"]
    L = 0.705 + 0.6 * (s["rev"] - 0.762)
    hid = 1 - 2.1 * (1 - s["tqa"])
    R = (0.98 * max(hid, 0.01) ** 4) ** 0.2
    print(f"{d:14s} direct {s['direct']:.3f} rev {s['rev']:.3f} style {s.get('style', 0):.3f} mc2 {s.get('mc2', 0):.3f} neigh {s['neigh']:.3f} tqa {s['tqa']:.3f} | L~{L:.3f} R~{R:.3f} score~{L*L*R:.3f}")
EOF

#101 Write/workspace/output/METHOD.md
# Method

**Summary.** I fine-tuned only the MLP weights (gate/up/down of all 32 decoder layers, 2.27B params).
Training used fact data generated from the public files, plus KL-to-base replay on general prompts. The
submission is a *model soup*: the uniform average of the weight deltas of independently trained runs (same
recipe, different data-order seeds), added to the base weights. Attention, Gated-DeltaNet, norms,
embeddings, vision tower and MTP head are bit-identical to the base checkpoint.

**Fact data.** All of it was built from the public fact files and corpus pages, plus outputs of the base
model itself (Qwen3.5-4B in vLLM, thinking off). For every fact, the best-matching lines of its source page
in `astro_corpus`/`news_corpus` were used as grounding context. Given the fact and that context, the base
model wrote:
- paraphrases and styled variants of the question (quiz, search-query, request, background-first, short);
- a declarative statement of the fact;
- short answer-first replies in its own style;
- "other-angle" and reverse Q/A pairs that ask for another detail of the same event (answers checked to
  appear in the statement or source);
- plausible same-type wrong answers.

Training items are single user turns with no system prompt, in the verifier's chat template with thinking
disabled (~38k in total):
- direct questions with answer-first replies (the World Cup set adds its 10 public paraphrases);
- A–D multiple choice in six layouts, with balanced letter positions, both random and plausible distractors,
  and replies that start with the letter;
- other-angle and reverse pairs;
- cloze and sentence completion;
- true/false.

**Replay with KL anchoring** (~25k prompts). Prompts:
- TriviaQA-train (12k) and NQ-open-train (6k) questions;
- Alpaca instructions (2k);
- ~5k "near-domain" pre-2024 quiz questions written by the base model about the injected facts' domains and
  entities, filtered to drop time-sensitive questions and questions that overlap the new facts;
- 300 thinking-mode SVAMP traces in the GSM8K prompt format, sampled with the GSM8K settings and kept only
  when correct.

The response text is the base model's own output (cut to 160 tokens). The loss on replay tokens is
forward KL(base || model) over the full vocabulary, against a frozen copy of the base. Facts use
cross-entropy on response tokens. Per-example mean, equal example weights. GSM8K (either split) and
TriviaQA-validation were never trained on.

**Optimisation (each run).** AdamW with fp32 master weights and bf16 autocast, LR 2e-5, 30 warmup steps,
cosine decay to 10 %, 1 epoch. That is ~720 steps of 64+ length-bucketed examples (~24 min on one H100).
One run used KL weight 2, the others 1.

**Selection.** Local held-out evaluation with vLLM:
- held-out paraphrases, styled questions, other-angle questions, and MCQs with random and plausible
  distractors (learning);
- held-out neighbor questions and base-correct TriviaQA-validation questions (retention).

Two public checks calibrated these local proxies:
- single full-MLP run with greedy-output SFT replay: 0.278;
- with KL replay: 0.365.

Things that were tried and rejected:
- LoRA (r=32): forgot less but learned the reverse/other-angle facts much worse;
- interpolating toward the base: lost learning faster than it gained retention;
- a stronger KL weight alone: no retention gain.

Averaging independent full-MLP runs kept their learning and reduced forgetting, so the final model is that
soup.

The file /workspace/output/METHOD.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

+++ new
**Optimisation (each run).** AdamW with fp32 master weights and bf16 autocast, LR 2e-5, 30 warmup steps,
cosine decay to 10 %, 1 epoch. That is ~720 steps of 64+ length-bucketed examples (~24 min on one H100).

**Final checkpoint.** W = W_base + (1/3)·Σ_i (W_i − W_base) over three such runs:
- data-order seed 0, KL weight 1;
- seed 1, KL weight 2;
- seed 2, KL weight 1.

The deltas were averaged in fp32 and cast to bf16. Only MLP tensors differ from the base.

[metadata] {"filePath": "/workspace/output/METHOD.md", "oldString": "**Optimisation (each run).** AdamW with fp32 master weights and bf16 autocast, LR 2e-5, 30 warmup steps,\ncosine decay to 10 %, 1 epoch. That is ~720 steps of 64+ length-bucketed examples (~24 min on one H100).\nOne run used KL weight 2, the others 1.", "newString": "**Optimisation (each run).** AdamW with fp32 master weights and bf16 autocast, LR 2e-5, 30 warmup steps,\ncosine decay to 10 %, 1 epoch. Only MLP tensors differ from the base.", "originalFile": "# Method\n\n**Summary.** I fine-tuned only the MLP weights (gate/up/down of all 32 decoder layers, 2.27B params).\nTraining used fact data generated from the public files, plus KL-to-base replay on general prompts. The\nsubmission is a *model soup*: the uniform average of the weight deltas of independently trained runs (same\nrecipe, different data-order seeds), added to the base weights. Attention, Gated-DeltaNet, norms,\nembeddings, vision tower and MTP head are bit-identical to the base checkpoint.\n\n**Fact data.** All of it was built from the public fact files and corpus pages, plus outputs of the base\nmodel itself (Qwen3.5-4B in vLLM, thinking off). For every fact, the best-matching lines of its source page\nin `astro_corpus`/`news_corpus` were used as grounding context. Given the fact and that context, the base\nmodel wrote:\n- paraphrases and styled variants of the question (quiz, search-query, request, background-first, short);\n- a declarative statement of the fact;\n- short answer-first replies in its own style;\n- \"other-angle\" and reverse Q/A pairs that ask for another detail of the same event (answers checked to\n  appear in the statement or source);\n- plausible same-type wrong answers.\n\nTraining items are single user turns with no system prompt, in the verifier's chat template with thinking\ndisabled (~38k in total):\n- direct questions with answer-first replies (the World Cup set adds its 10 public paraphrases);\n- A–D multiple choice in six layouts, with balanced letter positions, both random and plausible distractors,\n  and replies that start with the letter;\n- other-angle and reverse pairs;\n- cloze and sentence completion;\n- true/false.\n\n**Replay with KL anchoring** (~25k prompts). Prompts:\n- TriviaQA-train (12k) and NQ-open-train (6k) questions;\n- Alpaca instructions (2k);\n- ~5k \"near-domain\" pre-2024 quiz questions written by the base model about the injected facts' domains and\n  entities, filtered to drop time-sensitive questions and questions that overlap the new facts;\n- 300 thinking-mode SVAMP traces in the GSM8K prompt format, sampled with the GSM8K settings and kept only\n  when correct.\n\nThe response text is the base model's own output (cut to 160 tokens). The loss on replay tokens is\nforward KL(base || model) over the full vocabulary, against a frozen copy of the base. Facts use\ncross-entropy on response tokens. Per-example mean, equal example weights. GSM8K (either split) and\nTriviaQA-validation were never trained on.\n\n**Optimisation (each run).** AdamW with fp32 master weights and bf16 autocast, LR 2e-5, 30 warmup steps,\ncosine decay to 10 %, 1 epoch. That is ~720 steps of 64+ length-bucketed examples (~24 min on one H100).\nOne run used KL weight 2, the others 1.\n\n**Selection.** Local held-out evaluation with vLLM:\n- held-out paraphrases, styled questions, other-angle questions, and MCQs with random and plausible\n  distractors (learning);\n- held-out neighbor questions and base-correct TriviaQA-validation questions (retention).\n\nTwo public checks calibrated these local proxies:\n- single full-MLP run with greedy-output SFT replay: 0.278;\n- with KL replay: 0.365.\n\nThings that were tried and rejected:\n- LoRA (r=32): forgot less but learned the reverse/other-angle facts much worse;\n- interpolating toward the base: lost learning faster than it gained retention;\n- a stronger KL weight alone: no retention gain.\n\nAveraging independent full-MLP runs kept their learning and reduced forgetting, so the final model is that\nsoup.\n", "structuredPatch": [{"oldStart": 41, "oldLines": 8, "newStart": 41, "newLines": 14, "lines": [" ", " **Optimisation (each run).** AdamW with fp32 master weights and bf16 autocast, LR 2e-5, 30 warmup steps,", " cosine decay to 10 %, 1 epoch. #113 Write/logs/agent/sessions/projects/-workspace/memory/fact-injection-retention-lessons.md
---
name: fact-injection-retention-lessons
description: What moved the check score in the Qwen3.5-4B 2026-fact injection task (learning² × retention); measured results, 2026-11-03
metadata:
  type: project
---

Check scores on the 858-fact injection task (gpu5h scale, Qwen3.5-4B), 2026-11-03:
- MLP-only full FT, greedy-output SFT replay: 0.278. Hidden slices 0.58; retention is the bottleneck.
- Same, but replay as forward-KL to a frozen base over ~25k broad prompts (TriviaQA/NQ train, Alpaca, near-domain quiz questions): 0.365.
- Uniform soup of 2 such runs (different data-order seeds): 0.409. Learning *and* retention both improved. The 3-run soup was better again locally.
- Rejected: LoRA r=32 (local TriviaQA retention +6 pts, other-angle/reverse learning −19 pts), interpolating toward the base (loses learning fast), KL weight 2 alone (no gain).

**Why:** retention loss is from fragile base knowledge flipping under any sizeable weight change; averaging independent runs cancels idiosyncratic damage while keeping the shared fact learning.
**How to apply:** budget for 3+ seeds of the same KL recipe (~24 min each) and soup them, rather than tuning one run. Local proxy calibration: hidden-slice loss ≈ 2× local base-correct TriviaQA-val loss; learning tracks held-out other-angle Q/A accuracy. See [[qwen35-training-env-gotchas]].

File created successfully at: /logs/agent/sessions/projects/-workspace/memory/fact-injection-retention-lessons.md

The\nsubmission is a *model soup*: the uniform average of the weight deltas of independently trained runs (same\nrecipe, different data-order seeds), added to the base weights. Attention, Gated-DeltaNet, norms,\nembeddings, vision tower and MTP head are bit-identical to the base checkpoint.\n\n**Fact data.** All of it was built from the public fact files and corpus pages, plus outputs of the base\nmodel itself (Qwen3.5-4B in vLLM, thinking off). For every fact, the best-matching lines of its source page\nin `astro_corpus`/`news_corpus` were used as grounding context. Given the fact and that context, the base\nmodel wrote:\n- paraphrases and styled variants of the question (quiz, search-query, request, background-first, short);\n- a declarative statement of the fact;\n- short answer-first replies in its own style;\n- \"other-angle\" and reverse Q/A pairs that ask for another detail of the same event (answers checked to\n  appear in the statement or source);\n- plausible same-type wrong answers.\n\nTraining items are single user turns with no system prompt, in the verifier's chat template with thinking\ndisabled (~38k in total):\n- direct questions with answer-first replies (the World Cup set adds its 10 public paraphrases);\n- A–D multiple choice in six layouts, with balanced letter positions, both random and plausible distractors,\n  and replies that start with the letter;\n- other-angle and reverse pairs;\n- cloze and sentence completion;\n- true/false.\n\n**Replay with KL anchoring** (~25k prompts). Prompts:\n- TriviaQA-train (12k) and NQ-open-train (6k) questions;\n- Alpaca instructions (2k);\n- ~5k \"near-domain\" pre-2024 quiz questions written by the base model about the injected facts' domains and\n  entities, filtered to drop time-sensitive questions and questions that overlap the new facts;\n- 300 thinking-mode SVAMP traces in the GSM8K prompt format, sampled with the GSM8K settings and kept only\n  when correct.\n\nThe response text is the base model's own output (cut to 160 tokens). The loss on replay tokens is\nforward KL(base || model) over the full vocabulary, against a frozen copy of the base. Facts use\ncross-entropy on response tokens. Per-example mean, equal example weights. GSM8K (either split) and\nTriviaQA-validation were never trained on.\n\n**Optimisation (each run).** AdamW with fp32 master weights and bf16 autocast, LR 2e-5, 30 warmup steps,\ncosine decay to 10 %, 1 epoch. That is ~720 steps of 64+ length-bucketed examples (~24 min on one H100).\n\n**Final checkpoint.** W = W_base + (1/3)·Σ_i (W_i − W_base) over three such runs:\n- data-order seed 0, KL weight 1;\n- seed 1, KL weight 2;\n- seed 2, KL weight 1.\n\nThe deltas were averaged in fp32 and cast to bf16. Only MLP tensors differ from the base.\n\n**Selection.** Local held-out evaluation with vLLM:\n- held-out paraphrases, styled questions, other-angle questions, and MCQs with random and plausible\n  distractors (learning);\n- held-out neighbor questions and base-correct TriviaQA-validation questions (retention).\n\nPublic checks calibrated these local proxies:\n- single full-MLP run with greedy-output SFT replay: 0.278;\n- with KL replay: 0.365;\n- 2-run soup: 0.409 (learning 0.718, retention 0.793).\n\nThe 3-run soup was better than the 2-run soup on every local metric (held-out other-angle 0.79 vs 0.76,\nTriviaQA retention 0.88 vs 0.87, neighbor retention 0.83 vs 0.81).\n\nThings that were tried and rejected:\n- LoRA (r=32): forgot less but learned the reverse/other-angle facts much worse;\n- interpolating toward the base: lost learning faster than it gained retention;\n- a stronger KL weight alone: no retention gain.\n\nAveraging independent full-MLP runs kept their learning and reduced forgetting, so the final model is that\nsoup.\n", "structuredPatch": [{"oldStart": 60, "oldLines": 7, "newStart": 60, "newLines": 8, "lines": [" - 2-run soup: 0.409 (learning 0.718, retention 0.793).", " ", " The 3-run soup was better than the 2-run soup on every local metric (held-out other-angle 0.79 vs 0.76,", "-TriviaQA retention 0.88 vs 0.87, neighbor retention 0.83 vs 0.81).", "+TriviaQA retention 0.88 vs 0.87, neighbor retention 0.83 vs 0.81). #116
4h 06m

The submission is in /workspace/output/: model.safetensors and METHOD.md. It passes validate.py, and the public check on this exact file scores 0.422, with learning 0.732 and retention 0.787. The verifier scores a different set of facts, so its number may differ.

What the model is. It's the base model plus the average of the weight changes from three fine-tuning runs (a "model soup"). The runs used the same recipe with different data-order seeds. Only the MLP weights were trained; everything else is bit-identical to the base checkpoint.

Training data:

  • Fact data (~38k examples). For each fact, the base model was given the matching source-page excerpt from the corpus and asked to write varied rephrasings of the question, styled variants, reverse questions (ask for another detail of the same event) and plausible wrong answers. From these I built direct Q&A, A–D multiple choice with the letter first, cloze and true/false items. Everything uses the verifier's prompt format: no system prompt, thinking off.
  • Retention data (~25k prompts). TriviaQA/NQ train questions, Alpaca instructions, pre-2024 quiz questions near the facts' topics, and 300 thinking-mode math traces. Here the model was trained to match the base model's full next-token probabilities rather than just copying its answers. GSM8K and TriviaQA validation were never trained on.

How the score moved on the public check:

versionscorelearningretentionhidden slices
1 run, copying base answers as replay0.2780.6550.6480.58
1 run, matching base probabilities0.3650.7050.7340.683
average of 2 runs0.4090.7180.7930.750
average of 3 runs (submitted)0.4220.7320.7870.743

GSM8K stayed at 0.95–0.965 in every check (base 0.97). The main remaining weakness is forgetting of knowledge close to the injected topics: the hidden slices are at about 0.74 of the base.

Three things I tried didn't help:

  • LoRA (rank 32): it forgot less, but local accuracy on reverse questions dropped by 19 points.
  • Blending toward the base weights: it lost learning faster than it recovered retention.
  • Doubling the weight on the retention data: no retention gain.

I skipped a fourth run. It needed about 45 minutes and couldn't have been checked before the deadline, for a gain probably within noise.
